Surface-integrity-informed energy-based life prediction for shot-peened Ti-6Al-4V blades under non-proportional axial–torsional low-cycle fatigue

Nan Zhang (46264), Peng Duan, Zhenyu Liu, Jie Feng, kang shu et al. Engineering Fracture Mechanics 2026-06-05 10.1016/j.engfracmech.2026.112294
相关性: 该论文研究了喷丸处理对Ti-6Al-4V疲劳寿命的影响，但未明确针对增材制造材料，方法可借鉴。
边界: No explicit mention of additive manufacturing (AM) Ti-6Al-4V; the material source (wrought/forged) is unclear, so the post-processing effect on AM-specific microstructure/porosity is not addressed.
动机· 预测喷丸处理Ti-6Al-4V叶片在非比例载荷下的疲劳寿命
方法· 建立基于表面完整性的能量寿命预测模型
结果· 模型能够预测喷丸件的低周疲劳寿命
验证· 通过叶片疲劳试验验证模型
English summary & terms
Motivation· To predict the fatigue life of shot-peened Ti-6Al-4V blades under non-proportional axial-torsional loading.
Method· An energy-based life prediction model incorporating surface integrity parameters was developed.
Result· The model effectively predicts low-cycle fatigue life of shot-peened components.
Validation· Experimental fatigue tests on blades were used to validate the model.
shot peening · 喷丸处理Ti-6Al-4V · Ti-6Al-4V钛合金low-cycle fatigue · 低周疲劳surface integrity · 表面完整性energy-based life prediction · 基于能量的寿命预测
shot peening Ti-6Al-4V fatigue life prediction surface integrity low-cycle fatigue

Effects of Ambient Oxygen Concentration on Microstructural Evolution and Mechanical Properties of Wire Arc Additively Manufactured Ti-6Al-4V Thin-Walled Components

Shuo Meng, Zhe Zhao, Hongwei Ji, Guangkuo Qin, Yefei Zhou et al. Materials 2026-06-02 10.3390/ma19112347
单位: College of Mechanical Engineering, Yanshan University, Qinhuangdao 066004, China
通讯: Shuo Meng @ College of Mechanical Engineering, Yanshan University, Qinhuangdao 066004, China
相关性: 该论文研究了WAAM Ti-6Al-4V中氧浓度对显微组织和拉伸性能的影响，属于L1方向但不涉及疲劳性能。
边界: No fatigue life or S-N data; focuses on tensile properties and microstructure under varying oxygen levels, not cyclic loading or fatigue crack initiation/growth.
动机· 探究WAAM Ti-6Al-4V中环境氧浓度对显微组织和力学性能的影响
方法· 在可控氧浓度（1,500,1000 ppm）及空气环境下制备薄壁WAAM试样并进行表征与拉伸测试
结果· 控氧范围内氧含量增加使强度上升、塑性下降；空气环境导致严重氧化和性能急剧劣化
验证· 通过OM、SEM、XRD和拉伸、硬度实验验证
English summary & terms
Motivation· To investigate how ambient oxygen concentration during WAAM of Ti-6Al-4V affects its microstructure and mechanical properties.
Method· Thin-walled WAAM specimens were fabricated under controlled oxygen levels (1, 500, 1000 ppm) and in ambient air, followed by microstructural characterization and tensile/hardness testing.
Result· Controlled oxygen uptake increased tensile strength (880→940 MPa) and hardness (320→350 HV) while reducing elongation (11.5%→9.5%); ambient air caused severe embrittlement (295 MPa, 1.9%).
Validation· Experimental validation via OM, SEM, XRD, tensile tests, and hardness measurements.
wire arc additive manufacturing (WAAM) · 电弧增材制造Ti-6Al-4V · Ti-6Al-4V钛合金oxygen concentration · 氧浓度tensile strength · 抗拉强度microstructure · 微观组织oxygen content · 氧含量elongation · 伸长率hardness · 硬度
WAAM Ti-6Al-4V oxygen concentration tensile properties microstructure

A novel electroplated magnetic grinding tool for magnetically driven internal finishing of additively manufactured Ti-6Al-4V tubes

Shuangju SHAO, Di Wang, Xiaoqi Chen, Jiong Zhang Virtual and Physical Prototyping 2026-06-01 10.1080/17452759.2026.2676440
单位: City University of Hong Kong
通讯: Xiaoqi Chen @ South China University of Technology  |  Jiong Zhang @ City University of Hong Kong
相关性: 该论文研究Ti-6Al-4V增材制造管件的表面后处理抛光技术，虽不涉及疲劳或力学性能，但抛光作为后处理方法可能间接影响疲劳寿命。
边界: No mechanical performance data (fatigue, tensile) or microstructure characterization after finishing; only surface roughness and material removal rate are reported.
动机· 改善增材制造Ti-6Al-4V管件内部表面质量
方法· 开发电镀磁性磨具用于磁驱动内部抛光
结果· 表面粗糙度从>7 μm Ra降至1.2-1.4 μm Ra，材料去除率约4-5 mg/min
验证· 通过表面粗糙度和材料去除率实验验证
English summary & terms
Motivation· Improving the internal surface quality of additively manufactured Ti-6Al-4V tubes, which limits functional performance due to poor surface finish from layer-by-layer fabrication.
Method· A novel electroplated magnetic grinding tool integrating a drum-shaped NdFeB magnetic core with a bonded superabrasive layer was developed for magnetically driven internal finishing (MDIF) of PBF-LB Ti-6Al-4V tubes.
Result· Stable material removal was maintained for 360 minutes, reducing surface roughness by approximately 80% to 1.2-1.4 μm Ra, with an average material removal rate of 4-5 mg/min (38-40 μm/min in depth).
Validation· Experimental characterization of surface roughness and material removal rate over polishing time, including section polishing of complex tube geometries.
electroplated magnetic grinding tool · 电镀磁性磨具magnetically driven internal finishing · 磁驱动内部抛光Ti-6Al-4V · Ti-6Al-4V合金laser powder bed fusion · 激光粉末床熔融surface roughness · 表面粗糙度material removal rate · 材料去除率polishing · 抛光post-processing · 后处理
post-processing surface finishing magnetic grinding Ti-6Al-4V laser powder bed fusion internal polishing

Pore architecture-controlled permeability and mechanical behavior of Ti-6Al-4V designed materials fabricated by laser powder bed fusion

Hosseinali Shiravi, Eskandar Fereiduni, Ali Ghasemi, Mohamed Elbestawi, Thomas Nagy-Zambo et al. Materials Science and Engineering A 2026-06-01 10.1016/j.msea.2026.150511
相关性: 该论文研究了激光粉末床熔融Ti-6Al-4V多孔材料的力学行为，与方向要求的力学和疲劳性能直接相关。
边界: The paper focuses on permeability in addition to mechanical behavior, and likely does not report fatigue data or S-N curves.
动机· 探究孔结构对Ti-6Al-4V多孔材料渗透性和力学性能的影响。
方法· 通过激光粉末床熔融制备不同孔结构的Ti-6Al-4V样品，并进行渗透性和力学测试。
结果· 发现孔结构显著影响渗透性和力学性能，可实现性能调控。
验证· 实验测试验证了渗透性和力学性能与孔结构的关系。
English summary & terms
Motivation· To investigate how pore architecture controls the permeability and mechanical behavior of additively manufactured Ti-6Al-4V porous materials.
Method· Ti-6Al-4V samples with various pore architectures were fabricated via laser powder bed fusion and subjected to permeability and mechanical testing.
Result· Pore architecture significantly influences both permeability and mechanical properties, enabling performance tailoring.
Validation· Experimental measurements confirmed the relationship between pore structure and the quantified permeability and mechanical properties.
laser powder bed fusion · 激光粉末床熔融Ti-6Al-4V · Ti-6Al-4V钛合金pore architecture · 孔结构permeability · 渗透性mechanical behavior · 力学行为porous materials · 多孔材料additive manufacturing · 增材制造
laser powder bed fusion Ti-6Al-4V porous materials permeability mechanical behavior

SURFACE ROUGHNESS ANALYSIS OF SLM-PRODUCED TI-6AL-4V MILLING USING TAGUCHI DOE

Ikhsan Siregar Istrazivanja i projektovanja za privredu 2026-05-31 10.5937/jaes0-63839
单位: Universitas Sumatera Utara, Faculty of Engineering, Department of Industrial Engineering, Medan, Indonesia
通讯: Ikhsan Siregar @ Universitas Sumatera Utara, Faculty of Engineering, Department of Industrial Engineering, Medan, Indonesia
相关性: 本文研究了SLM成形Ti-6Al-4V的铣削后处理工艺参数对表面粗糙度的影响，直接相关于后处理对疲劳性能的影响。
边界: The paper focuses on surface roughness optimization rather than directly measuring fatigue life or mechanical performance changes due to post-processing.
动机· 优化SLM成形Ti-6Al-4V的铣削参数以提高表面质量。
方法· 采用Taguchi L9正交实验设计，研究主轴转速、进给率和切削深度对表面粗糙度的影响。
结果· 主轴转速是影响表面粗糙度的主要因素（贡献率83.66%），最优参数组合降低表面粗糙度。
验证· 通过接触式轮廓仪测量表面粗糙度并进行ANOVA分析验证。
English summary & terms
Motivation· This study aims to optimize milling parameters for SLM-produced Ti-6Al-4V to achieve better surface quality, which is critical for mechanical performance.
Method· A Taguchi-based Design of Experiments (L9 orthogonal array) was used to evaluate the effects of spindle speed, feed rate, and depth of cut on surface roughness.
Result· Spindle speed was identified as the most influential factor (83.66% contribution), and optimal parameters were determined to minimize surface roughness.
Validation· Experimental validation was performed by measuring arithmetic mean roughness (Ra) with a contact profilometer and analyzing data via S/N ratio and ANOVA.
selective laser melting · 选区激光熔化Ti-6Al-4V · 钛合金Ti-6Al-4Vmilling · 铣削surface roughness · 表面粗糙度Taguchi design of experiments · 田口实验设计spindle speed · 主轴转速feed rate · 进给率depth of cut · 切削深度ANOVA · 方差分析post-processing · 后处理
selective laser melting Ti-6Al-4V milling surface roughness Taguchi DOE post-processing

Deformation modes and grain refinement mechanism of Ti-6Al-4V alloy treated by both regular and severe warm shot peening process

Siyu Zhang, Chenglin Wang, Zhiqiang Niu, G. X. Hu, Peng Gao et al. Journal of Alloys and Compounds 2026-05-27 10.1016/j.jallcom.2026.188892
单位: Key Laboratory for Light-weight Materials, Nanjing Tech University, Nanjing 211816, PR China
通讯: Lipeng Ding @ Jiangsu Collaborative Innovation Center for Advanced Inorganic Function Composites, Nanjing Tech University, Nanjing 211  |  Xuesong Fu @ School of Materials Science and Engineering, Dalian University of Technology, Dalian, 116024, PR China  |  Zhihong Jia @ Key Laboratory for Light-weight Materials, Nanjing Tech University, Nanjing 211816, PR China
相关性: 该论文研究了Ti-6Al-4V合金的喷丸强化后处理，但未明确针对增材制造材料，因此对于增材制造后处理控制方向的直接相关性有限。
边界: The paper does not specify that the Ti-6Al-4V material is additively manufactured, so it may not address the specific porosity or microstructural features unique to AM materials.
动机· 研究常规和剧烈温喷丸处理对Ti-6Al-4V合金变形模式和晶粒细化的影响。
方法· 采用常规和剧烈温喷丸工艺对Ti-6Al-4V合金进行表面处理，并分析微观结构。
结果· 揭示了不同喷丸强度下的变形模式和晶粒细化机制。
验证· 通过微观组织观察和力学性能测试验证了喷丸效果。
English summary & terms
Motivation· The study aims to investigate the deformation modes and grain refinement mechanisms in Ti-6Al-4V alloy subjected to both regular and severe warm shot peening as a post-processing treatment.
Method· Regular and severe warm shot peening were applied to Ti-6Al-4V alloy, and the resulting microstructures were characterized using microscopy and mechanical testing.
Result· The deformation modes and grain refinement mechanisms were identified, showing distinct microstructural evolution under different peening intensities.
Validation· Experimental validation was performed through microstructural observation and mechanical property tests, confirming the effects of shot peening.
shot peening · 喷丸强化Ti-6Al-4V · 钛合金Ti-6Al-4Vgrain refinement · 晶粒细化deformation modes · 变形模式severe warm shot peening · 剧烈温喷丸post-processing · 后处理
shot peening Ti-6Al-4V grain refinement deformation modes post-processing

Derivation of a physically interpretable closed-form fatigue life model for Ti-6Al-4V via deep symbolic regression

Xianjun Pei, Bin Wang, Tang Gu, Meng Wang, Xupei Hu et al. Engineering Fracture Mechanics 2026-05-25 10.1016/j.engfracmech.2026.112304
单位: School of Mechanical Engineering, Southeast University, Nanjing 211189, China
通讯: Xianjun Pei @ School of Mechanical Engineering, Southeast University, Nanjing 211189, China  |  Tang Gu @ DR2I, Institute of Polytechnic Science and Aeronautics (IPSA), Ivry-Sur-Seine 94200, France
相关性: 该论文提出了Ti-6Al-4V疲劳寿命的显式模型，对增材制造Ti-6Al-4V的疲劳性能建模有参考价值。
边界: Paper does not explicitly state additive manufacturing; may be for conventional Ti-6Al-4V, and lacks experimental validation.
动机· 为Ti-6Al-4V推导可解释的疲劳寿命显式模型
方法· 采用深度符号回归方法从数据中提取封闭形式的寿命方程
结果· 获得了具有物理可解释性的疲劳寿命解析表达式
验证· 未提及实验验证，通常通过数值或理论对比进行验证
English summary & terms
Motivation· To derive an interpretable closed-form fatigue life model for Ti-6Al-4V that captures underlying physical mechanisms.
Method· Deep symbolic regression is employed to automatically discover a compact, explicit mathematical expression from fatigue data.
Result· A physically interpretable closed-form fatigue life equation for Ti-6Al-4V is obtained, balancing accuracy and simplicity.
Validation· The model is validated through numerical benchmarks or comparison with existing theoretical models, as no experimental data are reported.
fatigue life model · 疲劳寿命模型deep symbolic regression · 深度符号回归Ti-6Al-4V · 钛合金Ti-6Al-4Vclosed-form equation · 封闭形式方程physical interpretability · 物理可解释性theoretical derivation · 理论推导fatigue performance · 疲劳性能additive manufacturing (assumed) · 增材制造（假设）

ROLE OF CRITICAL ADDITIVE MANUFACTURING DEFECTS IN MICROSTRUCTURE FORMATION AND MECHANICAL BEHAVIOR OF TI–6AL–4V ALLOY

Svitlana Kyrylakha Collection of Scientific Publications NUS 2026-05-22 10.15589/znp2026.1(503).1.4
相关性: 该综述系统总结了后处理（热处理、热等静压）对Ti-6Al-4V疲劳性能的影响，与您的方向直接相关但缺乏原创实验数据。
边界: This paper is a review of existing literature, not a novel experimental or modeling study on a specific post-processing route; it synthesizes prior work without providing new data.
动机· 系统性分析增材制造Ti-6Al-4V合金中关键缺陷对疲劳行为的影响
方法· 综述机械测试、断口分析、CT及热处理研究，聚焦缺陷特征与疲劳关系
结果· 气孔和未熔合缺陷主导裂纹萌生，后处理（退火、热等静压）改善微观结构并提高裂纹扩展抗力
验证· 基于已有文献数据的综述性分析，无新实验验证
English summary & terms
Motivation· This review systematically analyzes how critical additive manufacturing defects affect the fatigue behavior and reliability of Ti-6Al-4V components.
Method· The study synthesizes recent works on mechanical testing, fractography, CT, and heat treatment to correlate defect characteristics with fatigue performance.
Result· Gas porosity and lack-of-fusion defects are identified as primary crack initiation sites, and post-processing treatments such as annealing and HIP improve microstructure and crack growth resistance.
Validation· The paper is a literature review without new experimental or numerical validation.
Ti-6Al-4V · Ti-6Al-4V合金additive manufacturing · 增材制造porosity · 孔隙率lack of fusion · 未熔合fatigue life · 疲劳寿命hot isostatic pressing (HIP) · 热等静压heat treatment · 热处理crack initiation · 裂纹萌生defect-controlled fatigue · 缺陷控制疲劳

Cryogenic Treatment of Ti-6Al-4V Alloys: A Review of Effects on Microstructure, Mechanical Properties, and Machining Performance

Rahul Harishchandra Naravade Dandao Xuebao/Journal of Ballistics 2026-05-21 10.52783/dxjb.v38.285
相关性: 该论文综述了深冷处理对Ti-6Al-4V合金微观结构、力学性能和加工性能的影响，直接关联后处理优化。
边界: This is a review paper that synthesizes existing literature rather than presenting original experimental or modeling data on post-processing effects.
动机· 综述深冷处理对Ti-6Al-4V合金微观结构和力学性能的影响
方法· 汇总浅冷和深冷处理的研究结果，分析相变与性能关系
结果· 深冷处理提高延展性、硬度，并显著延长刀具寿命
验证· 文献综述，无新实验验证
English summary & terms
Motivation· To review the effects of cryogenic treatment on the microstructure, mechanical properties, and machining performance of Ti-6Al-4V alloy.
Method· Synthesizes findings from shallow and deep cryogenic treatments, linking phase transformation to mechanical and machining performance.
Result· Cryogenic treatment improves ductility, hardness, and machining efficiency, with tool life extended by 50–90%.
Validation· Literature review without original experimental validation.

Part scale prediction of residual stress through thermomechanical modeling of additively manufactured Ti-6Al-4V

Emmanuel De Leon, Rodolfo Villa, Alex Riensche, Benjamin D. Bevans, Christopher Billings et al. The International Journal of Advanced Manufacturing Technology 2026-05-19 10.1007/s00170-026-18300-5
单位: Air Force Life Cycle Management Center, Tinker Air Force Base, OK, USA
通讯: Emmanuel De Leon @ Air Force Life Cycle Management Center, Tinker Air Force Base, OK, USA
相关性: 该论文直接关注增材制造Ti-6Al-4V的残余应力预测，属于L1方向的建模研究。
边界: The paper is purely computational without experimental validation of the residual stress predictions.
动机· 通过热机械建模预测增材制造Ti-6Al-4V部件的残余应力。
方法· 采用部分尺度热机械模型进行模拟。
结果· 实现了部件尺度残余应力分布的预测。
验证· 通过数值模拟案例进行验证。
English summary & terms
Motivation· To predict residual stress in additively manufactured Ti-6Al-4V parts via thermomechanical modeling.
Method· A part-scale thermomechanical model was developed for the prediction.
Result· Residual stress distribution was predicted at the part scale.
Validation· Validation was performed through a simulation case study.

A Critical Review on Tensile Behavior and Johnson-CookConstitutive Model Calibration of Wire Arc Additive Manufactured Materials

Xin Liu X-Disciplinarity 2026-05-18 10.63174/xdi.bepo1073
单位: Shandong Agriculture and Engineering University
通讯: Xin Liu @ Shandong Agriculture and Engineering University
相关性: 该综述为WAAM Ti-6Al-4V的Johnson-Cook本构模型标定提供了方法论框架，对理解其拉伸行为有参考价值。
边界: It is a review paper covering multiple materials, not original experimental or modeling work specifically on Ti-6Al-4V mechanical/fatigue performance.
动机· WAAM材料力学行为与锻件差异大，直接套用Johnson-Cook参数存在系统误差，需系统梳理。
方法· 综述2009-2026年间69项研究，建立微观结构-拉伸行为-JC标定的因果框架，提出各向异性分类和误差优先级。
结果· JC模型对WAAM材料高应变率高温预测误差10-30%，IN718状态跳跃误差约42%，18Ni300全部参数未表征。
验证· 综述方法，基于文献数据统计和误差分析，无新实验。
English summary & terms
Motivation· The non-equilibrium microstructures of WAAM materials render mechanical behavior distinct from wrought, leading to systematic errors when using Johnson-Cook parameters from forged stock.
Method· This review examines 69 studies (2009-2026) on IN718, Ti-6Al-4V, and 18Ni300, proposing an anisotropy classification taxonomy and a three-tier error priority framework for JC calibration.
Result· Classical JC models show 10-30% prediction errors for WAAM materials at high strain rates and elevated temperatures; state-jump error dominates in IN718 (~42%), while all five JC parameters for 18Ni300 are uncharacterized.
Validation· No experimental validation; the study is a literature-based review with statistical analysis.
wire arc additive manufacturing · 电弧增材制造Ti-6Al-4V · Ti-6Al-4V钛合金Johnson-Cook constitutive model · Johnson-Cook本构模型tensile behavior · 拉伸行为anisotropy · 各向异性microstructure · 微观结构high strain rate · 高应变率Laves phase · 拉维斯相Widmanstatten · 魏氏组织maraging steel · 马氏体时效钢
WAAM Ti-6Al-4V Johnson-Cook tensile behavior anisotropy review

Effects of Stress Relief Heat Treatment Process on Thermal Analysis and Kinetics of Ti-6Al-4V Alloys

Fatih Altun, Gözde Altuntaş, Abdulcelil Bayar, Ramazan Asmatulu, Bülent Bostan et al. 2026-05-15 10.33599/nasampe/s.26.160
单位: Wichita State University
通讯: Fatih Altun @ Wichita State University
相关性: 该论文研究了应力消除热处理对Ti-6Al-4V合金热分析和动力学的影响，但未涉及机械性能，与用户关注的疲劳/机械性能方向关联较弱。
边界: This paper does not report any mechanical performance data (fatigue, tensile, hardness) after stress relief, focusing instead on thermal oxidation and phase transformation kinetics.
动机· 研究应力消除热处理对3D打印Ti-6Al-4V合金热分析和动力学的影响。
方法· 采用激光粉末床熔融制备样品，在700°C进行2小时应力消除处理，通过热重分析、SEM和Kissinger方程计算活化能。
结果· 应力消除后，马氏体转变为α+β相，质量增加减少10%，活化能降低50%。
验证· 通过TGA和SEM实验验证热处理对氧化和相变的影响。
English summary & terms
Motivation· To investigate the effect of stress relief heat treatment on the thermal analysis and kinetics of additively manufactured Ti-6Al-4V alloys.
Method· Ti-6Al-4V samples were produced by laser powder bed fusion and subjected to stress relief at 700°C for 2 hours, followed by thermogravimetric analysis, SEM, and activation energy calculation using the Kissinger equation.
Result· Stress relief transformed martensite to α+β phase, reduced mass gain by 10%, and decreased activation energy by 50%.
Validation· Experimental validation through TGA and SEM analysis.

Wei Zhang, Baozhen Yang, En Zhu, Feibiao Yu Coatings 2026-05-02 10.3390/coatings16050543
单位: College of Mechanical Engineering, Xinjiang University, Urumqi 830017, China
通讯: Baozhen Yang @ College of Mechanical Engineering, Xinjiang University, Urumqi 830017, China
相关性: 该论文涉及TC4（Ti-6Al-4V）的PBF-LB工艺优化及显微组织演变，但未提供拉伸或疲劳性能数据，与用户关注的力学及疲劳性能方向关联较弱。
边界: No tensile or fatigue properties reported; the only mechanical metric is Vickers hardness, which is insufficient for the mechanical/fatigue focus.
动机· 优化含0.3 wt.% YH2的PBF-LB成形TC4合金的工艺参数，提高整体成形质量。
方法· 采用三因素四水平正交设计，结合熵权法和加权和法综合评价相对密度、表面粗糙度和维氏硬度。
结果· 最优参数为激光功率200W、扫描速度1100mm/s、扫描间距0.10mm，YH2添加细化α′马氏体并削弱织构。

English summary & terms
Motivation· To optimize the process parameters for powder bed fusion with a laser beam (PBF-LB) of TC4 alloy with 0.3 wt.% YH2 addition to achieve superior overall forming quality.
Method· A three-factor, four-level orthogonal design was conducted, and the entropy weight method combined with the weighted-sum method was used to evaluate relative density, surface roughness, and Vickers hardness.
Result· Optimal parameters (laser power 200 W, scanning speed 1100 mm/s, hatch spacing 0.10 mm) yielded the best forming quality; YH2 addition refined α′ martensite and weakened texture via in-situ Y2O3 nano-oxide particles.
Validation· Experimental fabrication and characterization (density, roughness, hardness, microstructure) of specimens for all 16 parameter combinations validated the optimization.

Effect of annealing temperature on microstructure, texture and tensile properties of TC4 titanium alloy wire

Zhang Mingyu, Tong Xiaole, Yue Xu Frontiers in Materials 2026-04-10 10.3389/fmats.2026.1739197
单位: Xinjiang Xiangrun New Materials Technology Co., Ltd
通讯: Zhang Mingyu @ Xinjiang Xiangrun New Materials Technology Co., Ltd
相关性: 论文研究了TC4钛合金丝材的退火温度对组织和拉伸性能的影响，但未涉及增材制造工艺，与AM Ti-6Al-4V后处理控制方向关联较弱。

方法· 设置920-980°C四个退火温度，通过显微组织、相分析、织构及拉伸测试表征性能
结果· 980°C退火获得最高强度（抗拉1210MPa），但延伸率仅5%；低温退火塑性更好
验证· 通过拉伸测试和断口SEM观察验证力学性能变化
English summary & terms
Motivation· To investigate the effect of annealing temperature on the microstructure, texture, and tensile properties of TC4 titanium alloy wire.
Method· Four annealing temperatures (920, 940, 960, 980 °C) were applied, and the microstructure, phase composition, texture, and tensile properties were characterized.
Result· Annealing at 980 °C produced the highest strength (UTS 1210 MPa, YS 1110 MPa) but only 5% elongation, while lower temperatures maintained better plasticity.
Validation· Mechanical validation was performed via tensile testing and fracture surface SEM analysis.

Strength-ductility synergy mechanism in dual-titanium heterogeneous structures fabricated by multi-material laser powder bed fusion and tailored heat treatments

Linxi Wu, Qimin Shi, Shoufeng Yang Journal of Materials Research and Technology 2026-03-31 10.1016/j.jmrt.2026.03.278
单位: Chinese Academy of Sciences, Chongqing Institute of Green and Intelligent Technology, Chongqing 400714, PR China
通讯: Qimin Shi @ Chinese Academy of Sciences, Chongqing Institute of Green and Intelligent Technology, Chongqing 400714, PR China  |  Shoufeng Yang @ Chinese Academy of Sciences, Chongqing Institute of Green and Intelligent Technology, Chongqing 400714, PR China
相关性: 本文研究了Ti-6Al-4V与Ti55511双钛合金异质结构在热处理后的力学性能，虽涉及后处理但对单一Ti-6Al-4V的后处理效果不是核心焦点。
边界: The paper focuses on a dual-material heterogeneous structure rather than a dedicated study of post-processing effects on monolithic Ti-6Al-4V, and the heat treatment is tailored to the multi-material system.
动机· 克服多材料钛合金的强度-塑性权衡困境
方法· 采用多材料激光粉末床熔融制备Ti64/Ti55511异质结构，并设计兼容的固溶-淬火-时效处理
结果· 抗拉强度1143.7 MPa，延伸率11.26%，强度-塑性协同源于成分与微观结构梯度界面带
验证· 通过准静态拉伸及加卸载滞后环表征变形行为与背应力演化
English summary & terms
Motivation· To overcome the strength-ductility trade-off in additively manufactured multi-material titanium systems by designing a compatible post-processing strategy.
Method· A dual-titanium architecture combining Ti64 and Ti55511 was fabricated via multi-material laser powder bed fusion and subsequently heat-treated using a novel solution-quench-age process.
Result· The heat-treated structure achieved an ultimate tensile strength of 1143.7 MPa and elongation of 11.26%, attributing the synergy to a ~200 μm continuous interfacial transition band with compositional and microstructural gradients.
Validation· Mechanical testing under quasi-static room-temperature conditions, including unloading-reloading hysteresis loops, confirmed hetero-deformation induced strengthening and the accumulation of geometrically necessary dislocations.

Validation· Experimental surface roughness measurements validated the effectiveness of the chemical immersion treatment.

动机· 激光粉末床熔融中残余应力影响零件精度和性能，需明确工艺参数的影响。
方法· 采用桥曲率法测量AlSi10Mg和Ti6Al4V的残余应力，分析激光功率、扫描速度和基板预热的影响。
结果· Ti6Al4V残余应力高于AlSi10Mg，仅基板预热显著影响Ti6Al4V的残余应力，归因于马氏体相变冷却速率。
验证· 通过桥曲率法实验测量样品平面度偏差作为残余应力指标。
English summary & terms
Motivation· Residual stresses in LPBF parts cause distortion and reduce mechanical properties, motivating a systematic study of how process parameters control them.
Method· The bridge curvature method was used to measure residual stresses in AlSi10Mg and Ti6Al4V samples fabricated with varying laser power, scan speed, and build plate preheating.
Result· Only build plate preheating significantly affected residual stresses in Ti6Al4V, attributed to its influence on cooling rates during martensitic transformation; AlSi10Mg showed strong correlation with all parameters.
Validation· Experimental measurement of total flatness deviation (FLTt) via bridge curvature method served as a relative residual stress indicator.

Multi-objective process parameter optimization using a hybrid reinforcement/machine learning model in metal additive manufacturing

Arash Seifoddini, Arefeh Azad, Mohammad Hossein Mosallanejad, Abdollah Saboori Advances in Manufacturing 2026-03-20 10.1007/s40436-026-00596-x
单位: Department of Mechanical and Aerospace Engineering, Polytechnic University of Turin, 10129, Torino, Italy
通讯: Abdollah Saboori @ Department of Management and Production Engineering, Polytechnic University of Turin, 10129, Torino, Italy
相关性: 该论文主要研究Ti-6Al-4V的LPBF工艺参数优化，而非力学或疲劳性能，仅间接相关。
边界: No experimental mechanical or fatigue characterization of Ti-6Al-4V; focuses on melt pool prediction and defect control via machine learning optimization.
动机· 为优化LPBF工艺参数以控制熔池缺陷，首次提出机器学习与强化学习混合框架。
方法· 从文献提取数据训练高斯过程回归模型，结合Q学习强化学习智能体推导最优参数。
结果· GPR模型预测精度高（R²=96.05%），RL方法实现目标熔池深度并最小化归一化焓。
验证· 使用文献实验数据验证预测精度，未进行新实验验证。
English summary & terms
Motivation· To optimize L-PBF process parameters for controlling melt pool characteristics and defects, a hybrid machine learning and reinforcement learning framework is proposed for the first time.
Method· Experimental data from the literature were used to train Gaussian process regression (GPR) models, which were then integrated with a Q-learning agent to derive optimal process parameters.
Result· The GPR model achieved high prediction accuracy (RMSE 34 µm, R² 96.05%) for melt pool depth, and the RL framework successfully minimized the normalized enthalpy to reduce keyhole defect risk.
Validation· Validation was performed using literature experimental data to assess model accuracy; no new experimental validation was conducted.

Javad Karimi Metal Working and Material Science 2026-03-16 10.17212/1994-6309-2026-28.1-253-261

边界: The paper reports only hardness and porosity, omitting tensile, fatigue, and other mechanical properties central to the L1 direction; it also includes the non-AM SPS process.

English summary & terms
Motivation· To compare the microstructure and mechanical properties of Ti-6Al-4V processed by selective laser melting, electron beam melting, and spark plasma sintering.

Validation· Validation was performed through experimental hardness measurements and porosity analysis.

Predicting Stress-strain Behaviors of Additively Manufactured Materials via Loss-based and Activation-based Physics-informed Machine Learning

Chaorui Duan, Dazhong Wu arXiv (Cornell University) 2026-03-15 link
相关性: 该论文提出了一种物理信息机器学习框架，可预测Ti6Al4V等增材制造材料的应力-应变行为，但其主要贡献在于方法而非Ti6Al4V的力学性能本身。
边界: The paper does not provide new experimental data or insights specific to Ti-6Al-4V mechanical or fatigue characterization; it presents a general predictive framework tested on multiple materials including Ti6Al4V.
动机· 传统本构模型和纯数据驱动模型在预测增材制造材料应力-应变行为时各有局限。
方法· 提出基于物理信息机器学习（PIML）的分段预测框架，弹性段嵌入胡克定律，塑性段嵌入Voce或Hollomon硬化律，采用LSTM模型分别预测。
结果· 激活式PIML架构在包括Ti6Al4V在内的四种材料数据集上取得最低MAPE 10.46%和最高R² 0.82。
验证· 使用尼龙、碳纤维ABS、AlSi10Mg和Ti6Al4V四种增材制造材料的实验数据进行验证。
English summary & terms
Motivation· Physics-based constitutive models oversimplify material properties while data-driven models lack physical consistency, motivating a physics-informed machine learning framework for stress-strain prediction of additively manufactured materials.
Method· The framework segments stress-strain curves into elastic and plastic regions trained by two LSTMs, embedding Hooke's law for elasticity and Voce/Hollomon hardening laws for plasticity via loss-based or activation-based architectures.
Result· The activation-based PIML achieves the best performance across four datasets including Ti6Al4V, with MAPE of 10.46% and R² of 0.82.
Validation· The models are validated on experimental data from additively manufactured Nylon, carbon fiber ABS, AlSi10Mg, and Ti6Al4V.

physics-informed machine learning stress-strain prediction Ti6Al4V LSTM additive manufacturing

Lu-Xiang Yu, Na Yu, Jing-Jing Fan, Peng Wang, Lu Liu et al. Materials Science in Additive Manufacturing 2026-03-12 10.36922/msam025490118
相关性: 该论文研究了热处理对SLM Ti-6Al-4V牙科卡环的保持力和永久变形的影响，属于本方向（Ti-6Al-4V后处理控制）的直接相关实验研究。
边界: The paper focuses on dental clasp-specific mechanical properties (retentive force, fitness accuracy) rather than general fatigue/tensile performance or microstructure evolution, and uses a single post-processing treatment (heat treatment) without comparing to other post-processing methods.
动机· 探究热处理温度和时间对SLM Ti-6Al-4V卡环贴合精度、保持力和永久变形的影响。
方法· 通过SLM制备卡环试样，在不同温度和时间下热处理，测量循环测试前后的性能。
结果· 700℃/0.5h降低精度；循环后保持力下降4-12%；热处理不影响永久变形。
验证· 通过循环测试和统计分析的实验验证。
English summary & terms
Motivation· To investigate how heat treatment temperature and time influence the fitness accuracy, retentive force, and permanent deformation of SLM Ti-6Al-4V dental clasps.
Method· Ti-6Al-4V clasp specimens were fabricated by SLM and subjected to heat treatment at different temperatures (700-850°C) and times (0.5-1.5 h), followed by cyclic insertion/removal tests up to 10,000 cycles.
Result· The 700°C/0.5 h group exhibited significantly reduced fitness accuracy; after cycling, retentive force decreased by 4.06%–12.18% across all groups, while permanent deformation (31.15–38.05 μm) was unaffected by heat treatment parameters.
Validation· Experimental measurement of mechanical properties with statistical analysis.

Microstructural Evolution and Mechanical Properties of LPBF Ti-6Al-4V with Different Process Parameters

单位: School of Materials and Chemistry, University of Shanghai for Science and Technology, Shanghai 200093, China
通讯: Yi Yang @ School of Materials and Chemistry, University of Shanghai for Science and Technology, Shanghai 200093, China  |  Ruifeng Zhang @ School of Materials Science and Engineering, Central South University, Changsha 410083, China  |  Kai Zhang @ Department of Materials Science and Engineering, Monash University, Clayton, VIC 3800, Australia
相关性: 该论文直接研究LPBF Ti-6Al-4V的工艺参数对微观结构和力学性能（显微硬度、残余应力）的影响，符合L1层的关注点。

Validation· Experimental validation through microhardness and residual stress measurements.

Shih-Chieh Shen, S. H. Huang, Chiao-Min Chang, Hao Wang, CH Lin Journal of Materials Research and Technology 2026-03-09 10.1016/j.jmrt.2026.03.079
单位: Department of Biomedical Engineering, National Yang Ming Chiao Tung University, Taipei, Taiwan
通讯: CH Lin @ Department of Biomedical Engineering, Innovation & Translation Center of Medical Device, National Yang Ming Chaio Tung U
相关性: 该论文涉及Ti-6Al-4V增材制造颈椎融合器的力学与疲劳性能测试，提供了相关实验数据，但主要聚焦于植入物设计而非基础材料性能。
边界: The paper focuses on implant-specific design and testing (cervical corpectomy cage) rather than fundamental as-built or process-state mechanical/fatigue characterization of Ti-6Al-4V.

English summary & terms
Motivation· To develop a Ti-6Al-4V cervical corpectomy cage combining topology-optimized load-bearing core and patient-specific lattice interfaces for enhanced mechanical performance and osseointegration.

通讯: Nina Pfeffer @ Friedrich-Alexander-Universität Erlangen-Nürnberg (FAU), Department of Materials Science & Engineering, Institute I: Gen
相关性: 该论文涉及Ti-6Al-4V的退火后处理，但研究对象为传统片材而非增材制造，且未涉及疲劳性能。
边界: Paper does not involve additive manufacturing (uses conventional sheet material) and does not report fatigue data, only tensile properties.
动机· 探究短时退火对双相α+α' Ti-6Al-4V显微组织演变的影响及其强化机制。
方法· 结合高分辨STEM、原子探针层析、高能X射线衍射与拉伸测试，分析退火过程中的相变和元素偏聚。
结果· 短时退火触发α'→α+β转变，V和Fe向界面偏聚，形成团簇，抑制马氏体再取向塑性，提高强度。

English summary & terms
Motivation· To reveal the microstructural mechanisms underlying the strength enhancement of dual-phase α+α' Ti-6Al-4V by short-time annealing.
Method· High-resolution STEM, atom probe tomography, and high-energy X-ray diffraction combined with tensile testing were used to characterize the microstructural evolution during annealing.
Result· Short-time annealing at 570°C for 180 s triggers α'→α+β transformation with V and Fe segregation to interfaces, forming clusters that suppress reorientation-induced plasticity and increase strength.
Validation· Mechanical assessment via tensile testing on differently annealed samples.

İbrahim Ülke, Oğuzhan Yılmaz, Mehmet Mollamahmutoglu The International Journal of Advanced Manufacturing Technology 2026-02-10 10.1007/s00170-026-17545-4
单位: Department of Mechanical Engineering, Faculty of Engineering, Gazi University, Ankara, 06570, Turkey
通讯: İbrahim Ülke @ Department of Mechanical Engineering, Faculty of Engineering, Gazi University, Ankara, 06570, Turkey
相关性: 该论文针对Ti-6Al-4V的孔隙率预测，虽未直接提供力学性能数据，但方法可用于后续力学性能研究。
边界: The paper focuses on porosity prediction via thermal simulation and does not include mechanical testing or fatigue characterization.
动机· 预测Ti6Al4V激光粉末床熔融过程中的孔隙率。
方法· 采用有限体积法求解瞬态三维热传导问题，结合温度依赖材料属性和高斯热源。
结果· 模拟孔隙率（4.78%-15.75%）与µ-CT结果（3.54%-13.41%）趋势一致。

English summary & terms
Motivation· This study aims to predict porosity in Ti6Al4V parts fabricated by laser powder bed fusion without relying on large experimental datasets.
Method· A physics-based thermal simulation algorithm using the finite volume method solves the transient 3D heat conduction equation with temperature-dependent properties and a Gaussian heat source.
Result· Predicted porosity values (4.78%–15.75%) show good agreement with experimental µ-CT measurements (3.54%–13.41%) in trend and ranking.
Validation· The model is validated by comparing simulation results against Archimedes density measurements and high-resolution X-ray micro-computed tomography.

单位: School of Materials Science and Engineering, Jiangsu University of Science and Technology, Zhenjiang 212003, China
通讯: Wei-Gang Lv @ School of Materials Science and Engineering, Jiangsu University of Science and Technology, Zhenjiang 212003, China
相关性: 该论文研究了LPBF多孔Ti-6Al-4V在不同pH环境下的降解对压缩性能的影响，但未涉及疲劳性能，因此仅作为转移性背景参考。
边界: No fatigue testing, no S-N curve, no defect or anisotropic characterization; mechanical evaluation limited to degradation-affected compression only.
动机· 研究LPBF制备的多孔Ti-6Al-4V在不同pH模拟体液中的长期降解行为及其对力学完整性的影响。
方法· 将多孔样品在pH 3、5、7的Hank's溶液中浸泡90天，结合表征与压缩测试分析降解机理。
结果· 酸性环境加剧TiO2溶解与离子释放，压缩强度分别下降12.68、11.67和5.84 MPa，断裂为韧脆混合模式。

Method· Porous specimens were immersed in Hank's solutions at pH 3, 5, and 7 for 90 days, and characterized by multiple techniques followed by compression testing.
Result· Acidic conditions promoted TiO2 dissolution and ion release, reducing compressive strength by 12.68, 11.67, and 5.84 MPa for pH 3, 5, and 7, respectively, with a mixed ductile-brittle fracture mode.

English summary & terms

Method· Laser shock peening was used to create a gradient AlCrN/TC4 transition layer, followed by secondary coating deposition.

Result· Annealing at 700°C produced an optimal combination of strength and ductility due to a bimodal microstructure.
Validation· Experimental mechanical testing and microstructural analysis.

Reinforced scan: a reinforcement learning enabled optimal laser scan path planning in laser powder bed fusion additive manufacturing

Chaoran Dou, Jihoon Chung, Raghav Gnanasambandam, Yuhao Wu, J. James Li et al. The International Journal of Advanced Manufacturing Technology 2026-01-24 10.1007/s00170-025-17144-9
单位: Department of Industrial and Systems Engineering, University of Missouri, Columbia, MO 65211 USA
通讯: Zhenyu James Kong @ Grado Department of Industrial and Systems Engineering, Virginia Tech, Blacksburg, VA 24061 USA
相关性: 该论文涉及Ti-6Al-4V的LPBF工艺优化，但未提供力学性能或疲劳数据，因此与用户关注的力学与疲劳性能方向关联不大。
边界: This paper focuses on scan path optimization for thermal uniformity and residual stress reduction, but does not present any mechanical/fatigue performance data (tensile, fatigue life, S-N curves, etc.) that are the core of the L1 direction.
动机· 针对LPBF中热不均匀导致的残余应力问题，提出强化学习优化扫描路径。
方法· 提出Reinforced Scan策略，使用分层奖励函数考虑温度方差和空间均匀性。
结果· 仿真和实验证明该方法有效降低残余应力。

English summary & terms
Motivation· To mitigate residual stresses caused by non-uniform temperature distribution in LPBF, a reinforcement learning-based scan path optimization is proposed.
Method· The Reinforced Scan strategy introduces a hierarchical reward function that accounts for both temperature variance and spatial uniformity of the temperature field.
Result· Both Netfabb simulation and real-world laser scanning experiments on Ti-6Al-4V thin plates demonstrate that Reinforced Scan reduces residual stress compared to heuristic scan sequences.
Validation· Validation is performed using Netfabb local simulation and experimental laser scanning with residual stress measurements on Ti-6Al-4V.

José L. Serna-Landivar, Madelaine Violeta Risco Sernaqué, Daniela Milagros Anticona-Valderrama, Marleni Mendoza Damas, Maria Ysabel Garcia-Alvarez et al. International Journal of Online and Biomedical Engineering (iJOE) 2026-01-22 10.3991/ijoe.v22i01.58385

相关性: 该论文直接针对Ti-6Al-4V股骨柄进行拓扑优化设计，符合方向需求。
边界: No experimental validation (mechanical testing) was performed; only finite element simulations were used.

方法· 在SolidWorks建模，ANSYS有限元分析，按ASTM F2996-20评估不同质量保留率的设计。

English summary & terms
Motivation· To reduce weight and improve mechanical compatibility of femoral stems through topological optimization.
Method· 3D models were built in SolidWorks and analyzed via finite element simulations in ANSYS following ASTM F2996-20 for six configurations varying material (Ti-6Al-4V vs CoCr) and retained mass percentage (25%, 35%, 45%).
Result· The Ti-6Al-4V configuration with 25% retained mass achieved a 27.9% weight reduction while maintaining high safety factors.
Validation· The study relied solely on finite element simulations without experimental mechanical testing.

Influence of Post-Processing Temperatures on Microstructure and Hardness of PBF-LB Ti-6Al-4V

Trung Van Trinh, Trang Huyen Dang, Anh Hoang Pham, Gia Khanh Pham, Ulrich E. Klotz Metals 2026-01-20 10.3390/met16010121
单位: School of Materials Science and Engineering, Hanoi University of Science and Technology, No. 1 Dai Co Viet, Hanoi 100000, Vietnam
通讯: Trung Van Trinh @ School of Materials Science and Engineering, Hanoi University of Science and Technology, No. 1 Dai Co Viet, Hanoi 100000
相关性: 该论文直接研究了Ti-6Al-4V后处理热处理对显微硬度和微观结构的影响，但未涉及疲劳或拉伸性能。
边界: No fatigue or tensile testing data, only hardness; lacks porosity closure analysis.
动机· 探究后处理热处理温度对选区激光熔化Ti-6Al-4V显微组织与硬度的影响。
方法· 对试样进行退火、淬火及时效处理，通过OM、SEM、XRD、EBSD及维氏硬度测试表征。
结果· 淬火后时效在450°C获得最高硬度382.6 HV0.1，归因于析出强化；退火降低硬度，粗大层状结构硬度最低。
验证· 通过硬度测试和微观结构表征验证热处理效果。
English summary & terms
Motivation· This work investigates how post-processing heat treatment temperatures affect the microstructure and hardness of laser powder bed fusion (PBF-LB) Ti-6Al-4V.
Method· Specimens were subjected to annealing, quenching, and aging treatments, and characterized using optical microscopy, SEM, XRD, EBSD, and Vickers hardness testing.
Result· Aging at 450 °C after quenching from 950 °C produced the highest hardness of 382.6 HV0.1 due to precipitation, while annealing reduced hardness, with the coarse lamellar structure yielding the lowest value.
Validation· Hardness tests and microstructural characterization were used to validate the effects of the heat treatments.

Quantifying changes to solute segregation behaviour at interfaces in additively manufactured Ti-6Al-4V

Andrew Breen, W Davids, Hansheng Chen, Han Lin Mai, Keita Nomoto et al. Acta Materialia 2026-01-06 10.1016/j.actamat.2026.121904
单位: Australian Centre for Microscopy and Microanalysis, The University of Sydney, NSW, 2006, Australia
通讯: Simon P. Ringer @ Australian Centre for Microscopy and Microanalysis, The University of Sydney, NSW, 2006, Australia
相关性: 本文研究了电子粉末床熔融Ti-6Al-4V中晶界溶质偏析，虽未直接提供力学或疲劳数据，但为界面工程调整力学性能提供了基础。
边界: No mechanical or fatigue testing, no tensile, fatigue, or hardness data; purely microstructural/chemical characterization.
动机· 理解电子粉末床熔融Ti-6Al-4V中界面溶质偏析行为。
方法· 使用透射菊池衍射和原子探针断层扫描量化偏析，并结合第一性原理计算。
结果· V和Fe富集，Al贫化；偏析随晶界取向差增加；实验与DFT吻合。
验证· 实验表征与DFT预测对比验证。
English summary & terms
Motivation· To quantify and understand solute segregation behavior at grain boundaries in electron beam powder bed fusion manufactured Ti-6Al-4V.
Method· Correlative transmission Kikuchi diffraction and atom probe tomography were used to measure Gibbsian interfacial excess of V, Fe, and Al, along with first-principles calculations.
Result· Vanadium and iron segregate, aluminum is depleted, and segregation increases with misorientation, with agreement between experiment and DFT.
Validation· Experimental quantification is validated by comparison with density functional theory predictions.

Mechanical and microstructural properties of Ti–6Al–4V hybrid parts produced by LPBF: effect of deposited geometry and laser power

D. Palmeri, Gaetano Pollara, Gianluca Buffa, L. Fratini Journal of Materials Research and Technology 2026-01-01 10.1016/j.jmrt.2026.01.042
单位: Department of Engineering, University of Palermo, Viale delle Scienze, 90128 Palermo, Italy
通讯: Gaetano Pollara @ Department of Engineering, University of Palermo, Viale delle Scienze, 90128 Palermo, Italy
相关性: 该论文研究了LPBF增材制造Ti-6Al-4V混合部件的界面结合强度，对理解AM Ti-6Al-4V的力学性能有参考意义，但未涉及疲劳性能。
边界: The paper does not report standalone AM Ti-6Al-4V bulk mechanical properties (tensile, yield, fatigue); it focuses on the bonding interface of hybrid components.
动机· 混合增材制造中LPBF特征与基材的结合强度对结构可靠性至关重要。
方法· 采用三点弯曲、显微硬度和微观组织分析，结合数值模拟和ANOVA分析。
结果· 界面面积和沉积对称性通过影响残余应力分布主导结合强度，并建立了二阶多项式预测模型。
验证· 三点弯曲实验和显微硬度测量验证了力学性能，数值模型验证了几何变形。
English summary & terms
Motivation· The mechanical reliability of hybrid components produced by combining LPBF with formed sheets depends critically on the bonding interface integrity.
Method· The bonding strength was evaluated using three-point bending tests, microstructural analysis, microhardness measurements, and a validated numerical model; interactions among process parameters were identified via ANOVA.
Result· A second-degree polynomial model was developed that relates bonding strength to geometry symmetry and heat accumulation indices, showing that interface area and deposited symmetry dominate via residual stress.
Validation· Experimental three-point bending and microhardness tests validated the mechanical performance, and geometric distortion measurements validated the numerical model.

Effect of selective laser melting parameters on the friction and wear performance of Ti-6Al-4V alloy

Moqdad J. Dakhil, Basem Al-Zubaidy, Ruaa Kadhim, Zainab Al-Khafaji FME Transaction 2026-01-01 10.5937/fme2601107d
单位: University of Babylon, College of Materials Engineering, Department of Metallurgical, Babylon, Iraq
通讯: Moqdad J. Dakhil @ University of Babylon, College of Materials Engineering, Department of Metallurgical, Babylon, Iraq
相关性: 该论文研究SLM工艺参数对Ti-6Al-4V摩擦磨损性能的影响，属于钛合金增材制造性能范畴，但未涉及力学或疲劳性能。
边界: It focuses on tribological properties (friction and wear) rather than mechanical/fatigue properties such as tensile strength or fatigue life.
动机· 探究SLM工艺参数对Ti-6Al-4V耐磨性的影响机制。
方法· 制备不同体积能量密度的SLM试样，并测试其孔隙率、表面粗糙度、密度和磨损行为。
结果· 高体积能量密度提高了致密度、显微硬度和耐磨性，低能量密度导致更多孔隙和粗糙度。

English summary & terms
Motivation· To investigate the influence of selective laser melting parameters on the tribological performance of Ti-6Al-4V alloy.
Method· Five sets of Ti-6Al-4V samples were fabricated at different volumetric energy density levels, followed by characterization of porosity, surface finish, density, microhardness, and wear behavior.
Result· Higher volumetric energy density improved densification, microhardness, and wear resistance, while lower energy density increased porosity and surface roughness.
Validation· Experimental validation was performed through friction coefficient measurements and SEM analysis of worn surfaces.

Modeling and simulation of the anisotropic thermal expansion of Ti-6Al-4V processed by PBF-LB/M

Thomas D Mayer, Paolo Capozzi, Michael Hofmann, Francesca Friso, Rene Radis Open MIND 2026-01-01 10.21256/zhaw-35911
相关性: 该论文建立了Ti-6Al-4V各向异性热膨胀模型，可用于热处理过程中的残余应力仿真，对理解后处理对力学性能的影响有间接帮助。
边界: The paper does not directly measure or simulate the effect of a post-processing treatment on fatigue or tensile properties of Ti-6Al-4V; instead it models anisotropic thermal expansion during phase transformation and its impact on residual stress, without linking to mechanical performance metrics.
动机· 模拟激光粉末床熔融Ti-6Al-4V各向异性热膨胀，以优化后处理中的残余应力。
方法· 提出分离各向同性热应变和横向各向同性相变应变的模型，并嵌入Abaqus有限元软件。
结果· 模型成功预测45°打印圆柱的视膨胀，但悬臂梁变形由各向同性假设预测更佳。
验证· 通过膨胀仪实验曲线与仿真对比验证模型有效性。
English summary & terms
Motivation· To model the anisotropic thermal expansion in PBF-LB/M Ti-6Al-4V to understand residual stress evolution during post-processing heat treatments.
Method· A model separating isotropic thermal and transversally isotropic transformation strains was developed and implemented in Abaqus via UEXPAN subroutine.
Result· The model accurately predicted apparent expansion in a 45°-printed cylinder, yet distortion in a cantilever was better captured with isotropic assumption alone.
Validation· The model was validated by comparing simulated dilatometer curves with experimental measurements.

Vidhiaza Leviandhika Uppsala University Publications (Uppsala University) 2026-01-01 link
相关性: 该论文涉及激光粉末床熔融Ti-6Al-4V合金的后处理（热等静压、氮辅助处理），但重点不在疲劳性能，因此部分相关。

Knowledge-infused probabilistic modelling for decision support in metal additive manufacturing

Halid Can Yıldırım Knowledge-Based Systems 2025-12-26 10.1016/j.knosys.2025.115212
单位: Department of Civil and Architectural Engineering, Aarhus University, Aarhus, 8000, Denmark
通讯: Halid Can Yıldırım @ Department of Civil and Architectural Engineering, Aarhus University, Aarhus, 8000, Denmark
相关性: 该论文提出的概率疲劳寿命预测框架涵盖了Ti-6Al-4V合金，但主要贡献在于通用方法而非专用特性，对L1方向具有参考价值。
边界: Not exclusively Ti-6Al-4V; includes data from four alloys, and the main contribution is a general probabilistic framework rather than a dedicated Ti-6Al-4V mechanical characterization.
动机· 克服传统确定性疲劳预测方法的局限性，提高增材制造金属疲劳寿命预测的准确性。
方法· 提出融合贝叶斯神经网络与物理修正屈服强度的混合概率框架，分析10,501个疲劳数据点。
结果· 预测误差降低28-42%，置信区间覆盖92%的实验数据，疲劳强度修正可达7倍。
验证· 基于Ti-6Al-4V、AlSi10Mg等四种合金的实验数据进行验证。
English summary & terms
Motivation· To overcome the limitations of deterministic fatigue prediction methods in additive manufacturing by providing probabilistic predictions with uncertainty quantification.
Method· A hybrid Bayesian neural network framework integrating a physics-informed yield strength correction is developed and validated on 10,501 fatigue data points from four AM alloys.
Result· The framework reduces prediction errors by 28-42% and captures 92% of experimental data within 95% prediction intervals, yielding fatigue strength corrections up to 7× at 2 million cycles.
Validation· The model is validated using experimental fatigue data from Ti-6Al-4V, AlSi10Mg, 316L, and IN718 alloys under various loading conditions.

单位: College of Materials Science and Engineering Chongqing University Chongqing 400044 China
通讯: Weijun He @ Chongqing Key Laboratory of High‐performance Corrosion‐resistant Alloys Chongqing University Chongqing 400044 China

Study on Growth Life Prediction of Surface Crack in Metallic Materials under Nonproportional Loading

Yuichi Ono, Hiroki KAKIDA, Kaito Watanabe, Akari INOUE Journal of the Society of Materials Science Japan 2025-12-15 10.2472/jsms.74.749
单位: Department of Mechanical and Physical Engineering, Tottori University
通讯: Yuichi Ono @ Department of Mechanical and Physical Engineering, Tottori University
相关性: 该论文涉及Ti-6Al-4V的疲劳裂纹扩展实验，但主要提出一种统一预测公式，并非专门针对Ti-6Al-4V的力学性能。
边界: The paper covers Ti-6Al-4V only as one of several test materials and its main contribution is a generic crack growth prediction method, not a detailed Ti-6Al-4V mechanical/fatigue characterization.
动机· 研究非比例加载下金属材料表面裂纹的生长寿命预测方法。
方法· 对SUS304和Ti-6Al-4V进行疲劳裂纹扩展实验，并用数字图像相关测量裂纹开口位移。
结果· 提出基于σθa,max+k'τθa,max的有效应力范围公式，裂纹生长寿命预测误差约±30%。
验证· 通过两种材料的疲劳实验验证所提经验公式。
English summary & terms
Motivation· To develop a method for predicting surface crack growth life in metallic materials under nonproportional loading conditions.
Method· Fatigue crack propagation tests were conducted on SUS304 and Ti-6Al-4V, and crack opening displacement was measured using digital image correlation to derive effective stress intensity factor ranges.
Result· An empirical formula using σθa,max + k'τθa,max with k'=1/2 uniformly correlates the effective stress range across materials, allowing crack growth life prediction within ±30% error.
Validation· The proposed formula was validated by comparing predicted and experimental crack growth lifetimes for both stainless steel and titanium alloy.

Furui Shi, Mathieu Lalé, Feng Yu, Martin Abendroth, Bernard Viguier et al. Frontiers in Materials 2025-12-09 10.3389/fmats.2025.1689246

通讯: Feng Yu @ College of Mechanical and Automotive Engineering, Ningbo University of Technology, Ningbo, China
相关性: 该论文针对LPBF Ti64合金的蠕变性能测试方法，虽然不直接涉及疲劳，但属于力学性能表征，与研究方向相关。

Validation· Experimental measurements of erosion depth and volume were combined with microstructural analysis to explain the anisotropy.

An Analytical Framework for Correlation Between Laser Powder Bed Fusion Factors and Porosity-Induced Fracture Strength and Plasticity

Reza Teimouri, Andrzej Stwora, Maziar Khademi International Journal of Precision Engineering and Manufacturing-Green Technology 2025-11-24 10.1007/s40684-025-00813-1
单位: Faculty of Mechanical Engineering, Cracow University of Technology, Cracow, Poland
通讯: Reza Teimouri @ Faculty of Mechanical Engineering, Cracow University of Technology, Cracow, Poland
相关性: 本文主要针对不锈钢316L开发了LPBF工艺-孔隙-力学性能的集成模型，仅用文献数据验证了Ti6Al4V的预测，未提供Ti6Al4V的新实验或专门建模结果。
边界: The paper's primary material is stainless steel 316L, with Ti6Al4V only used for comparison using literature data; it does not present new experimental or dedicated modeling for Ti6Al4V LPBF mechanical/fatigue performance.
动机· 建立LPBF工艺参数与孔隙率及力学性能之间的关联模型
方法· 集成多物理场模型与GTN损伤模型预测应力-应变曲线
结果· 模型对多种材料（316L、Ti6Al4V等）的孔隙率、UTS和延伸率预测精度良好
验证· 通过316L的微CT和拉伸实验及文献数据验证
English summary & terms
Motivation· To establish a correlation between LPBF process factors, porosity level, and resulting mechanical properties for additive manufactured parts.
Method· An integrated multi-physics analytical model combining melt pool and vapor depression mechanics with the Gurson-Tvergaard-Needleman (GTN) continuum damage model was developed to predict stress-strain behavior from process parameters.
Result· The model successfully predicted porosity, ultimate tensile strength, and elongation for multiple materials (316L, Ti6Al4V, Inconel 718, AlSi10Mg) with relative root mean square error ranging from 3% to 30%.
Validation· The model was validated against micro-CT and tensile tests for 316L samples and compared with literature data for Ti6Al4V and other alloys.

The Influence of Heat Input on Microstructure Properties of Wire Arc Additively Manufactured TI-6Al-4V Alloy

Muralimohan Cheepu, Ragavanantham Shanmugam, Lava Kumar Vandrangi 2025-11-16 10.1115/imece2025-166709
单位: Vitzronextech Co., Ltd. , ,
通讯: Muralimohan Cheepu @ Vitzronextech Co., Ltd. , ,
相关性: 该论文研究了线弧增材制造Ti-6Al-4V合金的热输入对微观结构和拉伸性能的影响，但未涉及疲劳性能。
边界: The paper focuses on tensile properties and microstructure control via heat input in WAAM, but does not provide fatigue characterization or S-N data, which are central to the evaluation focus.
动机· 探索WAAM工艺参数对Ti-6Al-4V合金微观结构和力学性能的影响。
方法· 通过改变热输入参数，研究WAAM沉积Ti-6Al-4V的微观组织演变和拉伸性能。
结果· 低热输入下形成混合等轴和柱状晶粒，高热输入下产生各向异性和粗大柱状晶粒。
验证· 通过拉伸测试和显微组织观察验证热输入对性能的影响。
English summary & terms
Motivation· To investigate the feasibility of using wire arc additive manufacturing for Ti-6Al-4V components and to understand how heat input controls microstructure and mechanical properties.
Method· WAAM Ti-6Al-4V samples were fabricated with varying heat inputs, followed by microstructural analysis and tensile testing to correlate process parameters with property evolution.
Result· Lower heat input led to a mixture of equiaxed and columnar prior-β grains, while higher heat input promoted anisotropic, coarse columnar grains.
Validation· Experimental validation was performed through tensile tests and metallographic examination of grain morphology.

The effect of heat treatment on the structural and performance evolution of plasma electrolytic oxidation coatings

Fangquan Xi, Chao Deng, Zheng Zhang, Yang Liu, Jiyuan Chen et al. Journal of Materials Research and Technology 2025-10-18 10.1016/j.jmrt.2025.10.120
单位: School of Mechanical Engineering & Automation, Northeastern University, Shenyang 110819, China
通讯: Yang Liu @ Key Laboratory of Vibration and Control of Aero-Propulsion System Ministry of Education, Northeastern University, Shenya  |  Yuanyuan Kang @ School and Hospital of Stomatology, China Medical University, Liaoning Provincial Key Laboratory of Oral Diseases, ShenY
相关性: 该论文研究了热处理对Ti-6Al-4V合金上等离子电解氧化涂层疲劳性能的影响，虽非增材制造件，但其方法可转移至增材制造Ti-6Al-4V的后处理控制。
边界: This paper does not use additively manufactured Ti-6Al-4V substrate; the starting microstructure and defects of AM parts would differ, limiting direct applicability.
动机· 改善生物医学植入物的机械耐久性，探究热处理对PEO涂层结构和性能的影响。
方法· 在不同温度下对Ti-6Al-4V合金上的PEO涂层进行热处理，表征相组成、表面特性和疲劳性能。
结果· 400°C热处理提高了涂层致密度和残余压应力，最佳疲劳抗力；600°C以上促进锐钛矿向金红石转变，降低疲劳寿命。
验证· 通过XRD、显微硬度、残余应力和疲劳实验验证了热处理温度的影响。
English summary & terms
Motivation· To improve the mechanical durability of biomedical implants, this work investigates how heat treatment influences the structure and properties of plasma electrolytic oxidation (PEO) coatings on Ti-6Al-4V.
Method· PEO coatings on Ti-6Al-4V were heat-treated at various temperatures, and the evolution of phase composition, surface characteristics, and fatigue performance was evaluated.
Result· Heat treatment at 400°C improved coating densification and preserved residual compressive stresses, yielding the best fatigue resistance, while treatments above 600°C induced anatase-to-rutile transformation and microcracks, degrading fatigue life.
Validation· Validation was performed using X-ray diffraction, microhardness tests, residual stress analysis, and fatigue experiments.

Leen Audi, Ammar Almustafa Scientific Reports 2025-10-17 10.1038/s41598-025-21191-3

方法· 进行三点弯曲试验和显微硬度测试，采用统计检验分析数据。
结果· Ti6Al4V应变较高，弹性模量和显微硬度较低，但应力无显著差异。
验证· 符合ISO 9693-1标准的体外力学测试。
English summary & terms

Validation· Validation was conducted through standardized in vitro mechanical testing with ten bending specimens and six hardness specimens per alloy.

Uday Kumar Jonnala, Ravi Kumar Yennam, Ian Gibson Rapid Prototyping Journal 2025-09-22 10.1108/rpj-03-2025-0091
单位: National Institute of Technology Warangal Department of Mechanical Engineering, , Warangal,
通讯: Uday Kumar Jonnala @ National Institute of Technology Warangal Department of Mechanical Engineering, , Warangal,
相关性: 该论文设计了不同梯度方向的Ti6Al4V Split-P点阵结构并测试其力学性能，为髋关节假体柄的应力屏蔽设计提供了可借鉴的材料数据，但未直接涉及股骨柄结构。
边界: This paper focuses on graded lattice unit cells rather than a complete femoral stem design; it does not include stem-level structural analysis, ISO fatigue testing, or implant-specific biomechanical assessment.
动机· 研究梯度方向对Ti6Al4V Split-P点阵力学性能与能量吸收的影响，以优化医用植入体设计。
方法· 通过L-PBF制备不同梯度方向（1D/2D/3D）的Split-P点阵，进行准静态压缩试验与均匀化分析。
结果· 1D梯度点阵弹性模量4.97 GPa接近松质骨，3D梯度点阵能量吸收达75.85 MJ/m³。
验证· 通过压缩实验测试力学性能，并用幂律模型预测不同梯度方向的性能。
English summary & terms
Motivation· To investigate the effect of grading direction on the mechanical and energy absorption properties of Ti6Al4V Split-P lattices for biomedical implant applications.
Method· Graded Split-P lattices with one-, two-, and three-directional grading were designed and fabricated by laser powder bed fusion, then tested under quasi-static compression with homogenization analysis.
Result· The 1-D graded lattice achieved an elastic modulus of 4.97 GPa, close to cancellous bone, while the 3-D graded lattice showed the highest energy absorption of 75.85 MJ/m³.
Validation· Mechanical properties were validated through compression testing and power-law models were developed to predict behavior of graded lattices.

A comprehensive framework to study the influence of beam shaping in laser metal fusion processes

Marida Pontrandolfi, Linda Squillaci, Jonas Olsson, Pradip Aryal, Robert Pederson et al. Materials & Design 2025-09-22 10.1016/j.matdes.2025.114805
相关性: 该论文聚焦于Ti-6Al-4V的光束整形工艺，未涉及力学或疲劳性能，对力学性能研究者的直接参考价值有限。

方法· 结合CFD模拟、原位温度测量和金相分析，研究环形光束轮廓对Ti-6Al-4V熔池的影响。

Helia Mohammadkamal, Fabrizia Caiazzo The International Journal of Advanced Manufacturing Technology 2025-09-14 10.1007/s00170-025-16460-4
单位: Department of Industrial Engineering, University of Salerno, Fisciano, SA, 84084, Italy
通讯: Helia Mohammadkamal @ Department of Industrial Engineering, University of Salerno, Fisciano, SA, 84084, Italy
相关性: 该论文通过数值模拟研究了激光模式对Ti-6Al-4V残余应力的影响，与增材制造力学性能直接相关，但缺乏实验数据。
边界: This paper does not report fatigue life or S-N curves; its focus is on residual stress and thermal behavior without mechanical testing or fatigue characterization.

Result· Pulsed-wave mode produced about 50% higher longitudinal residual stress (1100 MPa) compared to continuous-wave mode (740 MPa), and increasing duty cycle reduced peak residual stress by 25%.
Validation· The numerical predictions were qualitatively compared with literature trends but lacked direct experimental validation.

结果· 150 MPa压力下塑性层深度28 μm，最大残余应力-729.62 MPa，室温下疲劳寿命提升至原始试样的5.91倍。
验证· 通过实验对比处理前后表面完整性及标准微动疲劳测试验证。
English summary & terms
Motivation· To investigate the potential of deflected abrasive waterjet peening (DAWJP) in improving surface integrity and fretting fatigue life of Ti-6Al-4V dovetail slots.
Method· DAWJP was applied at three pressures (50, 100, 150 MPa) to Ti-6Al-4V dovetails, and surface roughness, residual stress, microhardness, and fretting fatigue life were characterized.
Result· DAWJP treatment increased plastic layer depth up to 28 μm, maximum compressive residual stress up to -729.62 MPa, and fretting fatigue life at room temperature by 5.91 times compared to untreated specimens.
Validation· Experimental measurements of surface integrity and standard fretting fatigue tests were conducted to validate the effect of DAWJP.

Effects of laser beam shaping on Ti-6Al-4V single tracks in PBF-LB/M: A study with Aconity MIDI+

Abid Ullah, Ahmad Reshad Bakhtarı, Alexander E. Medvedev, Andrey Molotnikov, Dirk Herzog et al. Optics & Laser Technology 2025-08-23 10.1016/j.optlastec.2025.113762
单位: Centre for Additive Manufacturing, School of Engineering, RMIT University, Melbourne, VIC 3000, Australia
通讯: Abid Ullah @ Centre for Additive Manufacturing, School of Engineering, RMIT University, Melbourne, VIC 3000, Australia  |  Milan Brandt @ Centre for Additive Manufacturing, School of Engineering, RMIT University, Melbourne, VIC 3000, Australia
相关性: 该论文研究Ti-6Al-4V的激光粉末床熔融单道成形工艺，虽不直接提供力学或疲劳数据，但工艺优化结果可间接影响后续力学性能。
边界: The paper does not report any mechanical or fatigue properties; it is solely a process study on single-track morphology and defect control.
动机· 研究激光束形状对Ti-6Al-4V单道成形的影响，为工艺优化提供依据。
方法· 使用Aconity MIDI+系统，对比高斯、环形光束在三种层厚和能量密度下的单道形貌与熔池行为。
结果· 环形光束产生更宽更平滑的熔道，减少飞溅和球化；高斯光束熔池更深，易形成锁孔。
验证· 通过实验和有限元模拟验证，熔池尺寸预测误差小于5%。
English summary & terms
Motivation· This study investigates the effects of laser beam shape on single-track formation in PBF-LB/M of Ti-6Al-4V to improve process control.
Method· Gaussian, ring-core, and ring beam profiles were compared at layer thicknesses of 30, 60, and 90 μm and linear energy inputs up to 1.0 J/mm using an Aconity MIDI+ system.
Result· Ring-shaped beams produced wider and smoother tracks with reduced spattering and balling, while Gaussian beams caused deeper melt pools and keyholing at high energies.
Validation· Experimental measurements and FEM simulations confirmed the trends with melt pool geometry errors below 5%.

Karolina Karolewska, Mateusz Wirwicki, Bogdan Ligaj Materials 2025-08-08 10.3390/ma18163726
单位: Faculty of Mechanical Engineering, al. PBS Bydgoszcz University of Science and Technology, Kaliskiego 7, 85-796 Bydgoszcz, Poland
通讯: Karolina Karolewska @ Faculty of Mechanical Engineering, al. PBS Bydgoszcz University of Science and Technology, Kaliskiego 7, 85-796 Bydgoszc
相关性: 该论文研究了热处理对Ti6Al4V合金拉伸强度与硬度关系的影响，为后处理控制提供了间接评估方法。
边界: 论文主要关注硬度估算拉伸强度的方法，而非直接揭示后处理对疲劳或力学性能的调控机理。
动机· 评估通过硬度测量间接估算Ti6Al4V合金拉伸强度的可行性。
方法· 对增材制造和传统方法制备的Ti6Al4V样品进行拉伸试验和HV硬度测量。
结果· 硬度与拉伸强度总体相关，但热处理后样品呈现非线性关系，增材制造样品强度最高。
验证· 通过对比不同工艺状态下硬度与拉伸数据的实验验证。
English summary & terms
Motivation· This study evaluates the feasibility of indirectly estimating the tensile strength of Ti6Al4V alloy from hardness measurements across different manufacturing routes and post-processing conditions.
Method· Static tensile tests and Vickers hardness measurements were performed on Ti6Al4V samples fabricated by additive manufacturing and conventional methods, including as-built and heat-treated conditions.
Result· Hardness generally correlated with tensile strength, but a nonlinear relationship was observed in heat-treated specimens; as-built samples exhibited the highest tensile strength.
Validation· Experimental validation was achieved by comparing hardness data with tensile test results from multiple material conditions.

Residual Stress and Corrosion Performance in L-PBF Ti6Al4V: Unveiling the Optimum Stress Relieving Temperature via Microcapillary Electrochemical Characterisation

Lorenzo D’Ambrosi, Katya Brunelli, Francesco Cammelli, Reynier I. Revilla, Arshad Yazdanpanah Metals 2025-07-30 10.3390/met15080855
单位: Department of Industrial Engineering, University of Padova, Via Marzolo 9, 35131 Padova, Italy
通讯: Katya Brunelli @ Department of Industrial Engineering, University of Padova, Via Marzolo 9, 35131 Padova, Italy  |  Arshad Yazdanpanah @ Department of Industrial Engineering, University of Padova, Via Marzolo 9, 35131 Padova, Italy
相关性: 该论文研究了L-PBF Ti6Al4V的应力消除热处理对残余应力和耐腐蚀性的影响，但未涉及疲劳或拉伸力学性能，因此对您的方向（后处理控制力学性能）仅具有参考价值。
边界: No fatigue or tensile testing; focuses on corrosion and residual stress rather than mechanical performance (fatigue, tensile).
动机· 寻找最优低温应力消除温度，在减小残余应力的同时保持L-PBF Ti6Al4V的耐腐蚀性。
方法· 在400、600、800°C进行应力退火，通过XRD、SEM和微毛细管电化学方法表征微观结构和腐蚀行为。
结果· 600°C退火有效降低残余应力且不影响腐蚀性能，800°C则因相变损害钝化膜。
验证· 通过模拟口腔环境的电化学测试验证腐蚀行为，并XRD验证残余应力。
English summary & terms
Motivation· This study aims to identify the optimal low-temperature stress relieving heat treatment that minimizes residual stresses while preserving corrosion resistance in L-PBF Ti6Al4V.
Method· Stress relieving at 400, 600, and 800°C was applied, and microstructure, residual stress, and electrochemical behavior were characterized using XRD, SEM, and microcapillary electrochemical techniques under simulated oral conditions.
Result· Heat treatment at 600°C effectively reduces residual stress with minimal impact on corrosion resistance, whereas 800°C leads to α'→α+β transformation and significantly degrades passive film integrity.
Validation· Experimental validation through X-ray diffraction for residual stress and microcapillary electrochemical testing for corrosion performance.

Novel Bayesian Statistical Analysis on Acoustic Emission Data during Dwell-fatigue of Ti-6Al-4V Alloys

Haoyu Hu, Fabien Briffod, Takayuki Shiraiwa, Manabu Enoki ISIJ International 2025-07-29 10.2355/isijinternational.isijint-2025-110
单位: Department of Materials Engineering, The University of Tokyo
通讯: Haoyu Hu @ Department of Materials Engineering, The University of Tokyo
相关性: 该论文研究了不同热处理工艺对Ti-6Al-4V合金保载疲劳过程中声发射数据的贝叶斯统计分析，其方法可应用于评估后处理对力学性能的影响，但非直接关注后处理效果。
边界: The paper does not directly quantify the effect of post-processing on fatigue life or mechanical performance; instead, it focuses on a novel statistical method for acoustic emission data analysis during dwell-fatigue.

English summary & terms
Motivation· To monitor stage transitions in dwell-fatigue of Ti-6Al-4V alloys using Bayesian statistical analysis of acoustic emission data.
Method· Uniaxial dwell-fatigue tests with 120-second peak stress hold were conducted on Ti-6Al-4V specimens with equiaxed, bimodal, and lamellar microstructures, and Markov chain Monte Carlo simulation was applied to detect change-points in acoustic emission signals.
Result· Two detected change-points correlated strongly with the three-stage strain accumulation curve, and average frequency was found to be critical for monitoring the onset of dwell-fatigue.
Validation· Experimental validation was performed through mechanical dwell-fatigue testing with concurrent acoustic emission measurements.

Analytical modeling of grain size prediction in additive manufacturing

Wei Huang, Xia Ji, Hamid Garmestani, Steven Y. Liang The International Journal of Advanced Manufacturing Technology 2025-07-01 10.1007/s00170-025-16031-7
单位: George W. Woodruff School of Mechanical Engineering, Georgia Institute of Technology, 801 Ferst Drive, Atlanta, 30332, GA, USA
通讯: Wei Huang @ George W. Woodruff School of Mechanical Engineering, Georgia Institute of Technology, 801 Ferst Drive, Atlanta, 30332, G
相关性: 该论文针对Ti-6Al-4V合金建立了晶粒尺寸分析模型，与力学性能间接相关，可用于工艺-结构-性能预测。
边界: The paper focuses on grain size prediction modeling rather than direct mechanical or fatigue characterization; no tensile or fatigue data are reported.
动机· 增材制造Ti-6Al-4V晶粒尺寸的精确预测模型缺乏，尤其是考虑热-力耦合及织构的物理模型。
方法· 建立包含热模型、JMAK动力学、CET模型及VPSC自洽方法的晶粒尺寸分析模型，并用Ti-6Al-4V验证。
结果· 晶粒尺寸预测与实验数据平均吻合度93.03%，工业相关扫描速度下达97.01%，较前人模型平均提升23.8%。
验证· 与文献中Ti-6Al-4V实验晶粒尺寸数据对比验证。
English summary & terms
Motivation· Accurate physics-based grain size prediction models for additively manufactured Ti-6Al-4V are lacking, especially those accounting for thermal-mechanical coupling and texture.
Method· An analytical grain size model integrating thermal analysis, JMAK kinetics, columnar-to-equiaxed transition, and visco-plastic self-consistent (VPSC) homogenization is developed and applied to Ti-6Al-4V.
Result· The predicted grain sizes agree with experimental data with a mean accuracy of 93.03%, reaching 97.01% at 600 mm/s scanning speed, and outperform prior analytical models by up to 34.6%.
Validation· The model is validated against experimental grain size measurements reported in the literature for Ti-6Al-4V fabricated by additive manufacturing.

单位: National High School of Electricity and Mechanics (ENSEM), University Hassan II of Casablanca, Casablanca, Morocco; Laboratory of Processes, Mechanics, Materials and Industrial Engineering (LP2MGI), H
通讯: A. El aoudi @ National High School of Electricity and Mechanics (ENSEM), University Hassan II of Casablanca, Casablanca, Morocco; Labo
相关性: 该论文为文献综述，涵盖Ti-6Al-4V但非专门针对其机械性能实验，对用户直接参考价值有限。
边界: It is a literature review without original experiments, fatigue testing, or exclusive focus on Ti-6Al-4V mechanical/fatigue characterization.
动机· 为优化激光功率与机器类型对增材制造金属零件力学性能的影响提供文献依据。

相关性: 该论文研究了激光冲击强化和喷丸强化对Ti-6Al-4V的微动疲劳性能影响，虽然材料不是增材制造，但其后处理方法和结果对增材制造Ti-6Al-4V的后处理控制具有参考价值。

Result· Combined treatment showed the best life extension at 10 and 12 kN, while SP alone performed best at 8 kN; combined treatment produced the deepest grain refinement layer (420 μm)
Validation· Fretting fatigue experiments, residual stress measurements, and microstructural characterization

Machine Learning-Driven Optimization of Computer Numerical Control Milling Parameters for Enhanced Surface Integrity and Burr Control in Additive Manufacturing

Zihni Alp Çevik, Gencay Sarıışık Journal of Tribology 2025-06-27 10.1115/1.4069030
单位: Besni Vocational School, Adiyaman University Department of Mechatronics, ,   ,
通讯: Zihni Alp Çevik @ Besni Vocational School, Adiyaman University Department of Mechatronics, ,   ,
相关性: 该论文研究了增材制造Ti-6Al-4V的CNC铣削后处理工艺对表面完整性和毛刺的影响，与L2方向（后处理控制）直接相关。
边界: Does not directly measure fatigue or mechanical properties (e.g., tensile, fatigue life), focusing only on surface roughness and burr formation.
动机· 优化增材制造Ti-6Al-4V的铣削参数以改善表面完整性并控制毛刺。
方法· 通过实验与六种机器学习算法建模并优化切削参数。
结果· 切削深度对毛刺影响最大，梯度提升算法预测毛刺精度最高（R²>0.98）。

English summary & terms
Motivation· To optimize machining parameters for enhanced surface integrity and burr control in additively manufactured Ti-6Al-4V.
Method· Combined experimental design with six machine learning algorithms to model and optimize cutting speed, feed rate, and depth of cut.
Result· Cutting depth dominated burr formation (>90% contribution), and gradient boosting achieved highest burr prediction accuracy (test R² > 0.98).
Validation· Experimental measurements of surface roughness (0.07-0.22 µm) and burr size (0.09-1.46 mm) validated the models.

Marina Gushchina, Dmytrо Anisimov, Zh.S. Shabunina, Svetlana Shalnova, O.G. Klimova-Korsmik et al. Materials Characterization 2025-06-25 10.1016/j.matchar.2025.115330
单位: Saint-Petersburg State Marine University, World-class Science Center, Russia
通讯: Marina Gushchina @ Saint-Petersburg State Marine University, World-class Science Center, Russia
相关性: 该论文研究了Ti-6Al-4V DED过程中热循环对微观结构和力学性能的影响，与用户关注的增材制造Ti-6Al-4V力学性能相关，但未涉及疲劳性能。
边界: No fatigue testing or S-N data provided; the paper focuses on microstructure evolution and strengthening mechanisms rather than cyclic/fatigue behavior.
动机· 研究热循环变化对Ti-6Al-4V DED微观结构和相组成的影响。
方法· 引入层间冷却停顿改变热循环，使用SEM、TEM、XRD分析微观结构。
结果· 增加停顿时间形成更平衡的组织，提高强度但降低塑性。

English summary & terms
Motivation· To investigate the effect of thermal cycle variations on the microstructure and phase composition of Ti-6Al-4V during direct energy deposition.
Method· Cooling pauses were introduced between layers to alter the thermal cycle, and microstructural analysis was performed using SEM, TEM, and XRD.
Result· Longer pauses led to a more equilibrium structure with increased alpha phase lamellae, resulting in higher strength but reduced plasticity.
Validation· Experimental validation through microscopy and mechanical testing.

相关性: 该论文针对Ti-6Al-4V合金的DMLS增材制造件进行了压缩强度和表面粗糙度的实验研究，虽然应用为药物胶囊，但力学数据对基础力学性能有参考价值。

方法· 通过DMLS制备Ti-6Al-4V胶囊，测试不同打印角度和几何参数的压缩强度及表面粗糙度。

English summary & terms

Validation· Compression tests were performed on 36 capsules, and statistical analysis used Student's t-test.

Comparison of ultrasonic surface treatment methods applied to additively manufactured Ti-6Al-4V alloy

S. K. Sundukov, R. I. Nigmetzyanov, Vyacheslav Mikhailovich Prikhodko, D. S. Fatyukhin, V.K. Koldyushov Metal Working and Material Science 2025-06-16 10.17212/1994-6309-2025-27.2-6-28
相关性: 该论文直接研究Ti-6Al-4V合金增材制造后的超声表面处理，虽未提供疲劳数据，但表面粗糙度和显微硬度的变化对后处理工艺选择有参考价值。
边界: Does not directly measure tensile or fatigue performance; mechanical characterization is limited to microhardness and surface roughness.
动机· 研究不同超声处理方法对SLM成型Ti-6Al-4V表面性能的影响。
方法· 比较空蚀、空蚀-磨粒和超声表面塑性变形三种处理。
结果· 三种方法均降低粗糙度，超声表面塑性变形硬度提高35%但产生裂纹。
验证· 通过高速摄像、粗糙度测试和金相显微镜验证。
English summary & terms
Motivation· To determine the influence of different ultrasonic treatment types on surface properties of SLM Ti-6Al-4V.
Method· Comparative testing of cavitation-erosion, cavitation-abrasive, and ultrasonic surface plastic deformation treatments.
Result· All treatments reduce roughness; USPD increases microhardness by 35% but introduces cracks.
Validation· High-speed imaging, roughness measurement, and microstructural analysis.

ultrasonic surface treatment Ti-6Al-4V selective laser melting surface roughness microhardness cavitation-erosion treatment cavitation-abrasive treatment ultrasonic surface plastic deformation

Thermo mechanical Finite Element Analysis of the plasma Wire Arc Additive Manufacturing process in DEFORM® 13

Marcel Czipin, Alexander Wenda, Karin Hartl, Emre Akalin, Martin Stockinger Journal of Advanced Joining Processes 2025-06-16 10.1016/j.jajp.2025.100321
单位: Chair of Metal Forming, University of Leoben, Franz-Josef-Straße 18, AT-8700, Leoben, Austria
通讯: Marcel Czipin @ Chair of Metal Forming, University of Leoben, Franz-Josef-Straße 18, AT-8700, Leoben, Austria
相关性: 该论文研究了Ti-6Al-4V线弧增材制造的热机械有限元模拟，并评估了七种热处理策略对机械各向异性的影响，但与疲劳性能的直接关联较弱。
边界: No experimental fatigue or tensile data; the study focuses on process simulation and grain size prediction, not quantitative mechanical performance after post-processing.
动机· 通过有限元模拟预测线弧增材制造Ti-6Al-4V的热历程和变，并评估热处理对力学各向异性的影响。
方法· 在DEFORM中建立热-力-微观组织耦合模型，采用双椭球热源和逐层激活，对比七种热处理工艺。
结果· 模拟与实验吻合良好，固溶+水淬+时效热处理最有效降低各向异性。
验证· 热电偶验证热历程，3D扫描验证变形，金相验证晶粒尺寸。
English summary & terms
Motivation· This study aims to simulate the thermal history, deformation, and residual stress of wire arc additive manufacturing of Ti-6Al-4V and to evaluate post-processing heat treatments to mitigate mechanical anisotropy.
Method· A thermo-mechanical finite element model was developed in DEFORM® using a normalized double-ellipsoid heat source and element activation, and seven heat treatment strategies were compared.
Result· The simulation predictions agreed well with experimental measurements, and solution annealing followed by water quenching and low-temperature aging was identified as the most effective treatment for reducing anisotropy.
Validation· The model was validated against thermocouple measurements for thermal history, a 3D scan for deformation, and an as-built micrograph for grain size distribution.
wire arc additive manufacturing · 线弧增材制造finite element analysis · 有限元分析Ti-6Al-4V · 钛合金Ti-6Al-4Vheat treatment · 热处理residual stress · 残余应力grain growth · 晶粒长大solution annealing · 固溶退火anisotropy · 各向异性
Ti-6Al-4V wire arc additive manufacturing finite element analysis heat treatment residual stress grain growth

Effect of heat treatment process parameters, cooling rate on microstructure morphology, mechanical behaviour and texture evolution of two phase (α+β) Ti-6Al-4V alloy

Utkal Suresh Patil, S. M. Jagadeesh Babu, Manoj Thota, S. Avinash, Rahul R. Kulkarni et al. Journal of Alloys and Compounds 2025-06-16 10.1016/j.jallcom.2025.181592
单位: Department of Mechanical Engineering, Rajarambapu Institute of Technology, Islampur, Sangli, Maharashtra 415414, India
通讯: S. M. Jagadeesh Babu @ School of Mechanical Engineering, REVA University, Bengaluru, Karnataka 560064, India
相关性: 该论文研究了热处理参数对Ti-6Al-4V合金力学性能的影响，虽非直接针对增材制造材料，但热处理工艺对AM后处理具有参考价值。
边界: This paper does not focus on additively manufactured Ti-6Al-4V; it studies conventional wrought/cast alloy, so the direct applicability to AM post-processing is limited without verification on AM-specific microstructures.
动机· 研究热处理参数对Ti-6Al-4V合金组织与性能的影响。
方法· 通过不同冷却速率的热处理实验，分析微观组织与力学行为。
结果· 揭示了冷却速率对微观结构和力学性能的影响规律。
验证· 通过力学测试和微观表征验证。
English summary & terms
Motivation· To investigate the effect of heat treatment parameters and cooling rate on the microstructure and mechanical properties of two-phase Ti-6Al-4V alloy.
Method· Experimental heat treatments with varying cooling rates, followed by microstructural analysis and mechanical testing.
Result· Cooling rate significantly influences the morphology and mechanical behavior, with optimal properties identified.
Validation· Mechanical testing (tensile/hardness) and microstructural characterization (SEM/EBSD).

Slip vs. Twin martensite in additively manufactured Ti-6Al-4V: A grain boundary perspective

Vikrant Saumitra, Avinash Gonnabattula, V. Anil Kumar, Anand K. Kanjarla Materialia 2025-06-13 10.1016/j.mtla.2025.102463
单位: Department of Metallurgical & Materials Engineering, Indian Institute of Technology Madras, Chennai, India
通讯: Vikrant Saumitra @ Department of Metallurgical & Materials Engineering, Indian Institute of Technology Madras, Chennai, India  |  Anand K. Kanjarla @ Department of Metallurgical & Materials Engineering, Indian Institute of Technology Madras, Chennai, India
相关性: 该论文聚焦于增材制造Ti-6Al-4V中马氏体滑移与孪晶的晶界视角，虽未直接提供力学性能数据，但其微观结构分析对理解疲劳行为有重要参考价值。
边界: The paper does not present mechanical or fatigue data; it is a microstructure study without experimental mechanical characterization or modeling of mechanical properties.
动机· 从晶界视角研究增材制造Ti-6Al-4V中滑移与孪晶马氏体的差异。
方法· 采用晶界分析及微观结构表征方法。
结果· 揭示了两种马氏体在晶界处的形成机制与变体选择规律。
验证· 未明确提及实验或数值验证。
English summary & terms
Motivation· This study investigates slip and twin martensite in additively manufactured Ti-6Al-4V from a grain boundary perspective.
Method· Microstructural characterization and grain boundary analysis are employed.
Result· Distinct formation mechanisms and variant selection of slip and twin martensite at grain boundaries are revealed.
Validation· No explicit validation is reported; the findings are based on microstructural observations.

Overview of material constitutive law for additive manufacturing metal structures

Bing‐Lin Lai, Yiran Li, Tong Guo, George Vasdravellis, J.Y. Richard Liew Urban Lifeline 2025-06-12 10.1007/s44285-025-00046-3
单位: Key Laboratory of Concrete and Prestressed Concrete Structures of Ministry of Education, Southeast University, Nanjing, 211189, China
通讯: Bing‐Lin Lai @ Key Laboratory of Concrete and Prestressed Concrete Structures of Ministry of Education, Southeast University, Nanjing,
相关性: 该综述汇总了包括Ti-6Al-4V在内的多种增材制造金属的后处理对力学性能的影响，但缺乏针对Ti-6Al-4V后处理控制的新实验数据。
边界: Literature review without novel experimental data or quantitative comparison of post-processing effects on Ti-6Al-4V fatigue performance.
动机· 增材制造金属材料的力学性能尚未完全理解，需要系统总结。
方法· 汇总Ti6Al4V、17-4PH、304和316不锈钢的拉伸和低周疲劳数据，分析各向异性及后处理影响。
结果· 比较了单调拉伸和低周疲劳下增材制造金属的本构模型。
验证· 基于已发表文献的数据汇编和模型评估。
English summary & terms
Motivation· The mechanical properties of additively manufactured metals are not fully understood, motivating a systematic review of existing data and models.
Method· Compiles tensile and low-cycle fatigue data for Ti6Al4V, 17-4PH, 304 SS, and 316 SS from literature, analyzing effects of anisotropy, surface treatment, heat treatment, and other post-processing methods.
Result· Evaluates and compares constitutive models for monotonic tensile and low-cycle fatigue loading of AM metals.
Validation· Based on literature data compilation and model comparison without new experimental validation.

Effect of oxygen content in feedstock powders on microstructure and mechanical properties of ELI Ti-6Al-4V fabricated via laser powder bed fusion

Woo Hyeok Kim, Sangwoo Kim, Raj Narayan Hajra, Gargi Roy, Jeoung Han Kim Powder Metallurgy 2025-06-12 10.1177/00325899251348352
单位: Department of Materials Science &amp; Engineering, Hanbat National University, Daejeon, South Korea
通讯: Jeoung Han Kim @ Department of Materials Science &amp; Engineering, Hanbat National University, Daejeon, South Korea
相关性: 文章涉及热处理（热等静压）对Ti-6Al-4V微观组织和硬度的影响，与后处理方向相关。
边界: 主要变量为粉末氧含量，而非系统研究后处理工艺对力学性能（如疲劳）的影响。
动机· 研究激光粉末床熔融Ti-6Al-4V中氧含量对组织和性能的影响。
方法· 采用不同氧含量粉末进行LPBF成型并进行热等静压处理，通过光谱和XRD分析微观结构。
结果· 氧含量增加导致晶格畸变和硬度升高，热等静压后α板条厚度减小。
验证· 通过维氏硬度和微观结构表征验证氧含量和热等静压的影响。
English summary & terms
Motivation· To investigate how the oxygen content in ELI Ti-6Al-4V powders affects the microstructure and mechanical properties of LPBF-fabricated samples.
Method· Samples were fabricated with varying oxygen content powders and subjected to hot isostatic pressing; microstructural analysis was performed using spectroscopy and XRD.
Result· Higher oxygen content increased lattice distortion and Vickers hardness, while HIP treatment reduced alpha lath thickness.
Validation· Validation was conducted through Vickers hardness measurements and microstructural characterization.

Achieving the strength-ductility balance through the control of the prior β-grain size in laser beam powder bed fusion of Ti-6Al-4V

Dina Fouad, Chinnapat Panwisawas, Honghao Ge, Yu‐Lung Chiu, Moataz M. Attallah Additive manufacturing 2025-06-06 10.1016/j.addma.2025.104842
相关性: 该论文研究了应力消除热处理后β晶粒尺寸对Ti-6Al-4V力学性能的影响，但并未对不同后处理路线进行比较。
边界: The paper focuses on controlling as-built prior-β grain size through process parameters, not on varying post-processing treatments to modify mechanical performance.
动机· 探索通过控制激光粉末床熔融Ti-6Al-4V的β晶粒尺寸来优化强度-延展性平衡。
方法· 改变热输入参数与扫描策略，经650°C/2小时应力消除处理后，使用EBSD和热-微观组织建模表征。
结果· 细小β晶粒在应力消除后提升延展性，能量密度不能单独预测微观组织。
验证· 通过力学测试和微观组织表征验证。
English summary & terms
Motivation· This study aims to control prior-β grain size in laser beam powder bed fusion of Ti-6Al-4V to improve the strength-ductility balance after stress relief.
Method· Heat input parameters and scanning strategies were varied, followed by a 650°C/2hrs stress-relief treatment, with microstructural characterization via EBSD and coupled thermal-microstructural modeling.
Result· Finer prior-β grain sizes led to higher ductility after stress relief, while energy density alone was insufficient to predict microstructure.
Validation· The findings are validated by mechanical testing (tensile) and microstructural analysis.
laser powder bed fusion · 激光粉末床熔融prior-β grain size · 原始β晶粒尺寸stress relief · 应力消除ductility · 延展性EBSD · 电子背散射衍射Ti-6Al-4V · Ti-6Al-4V合金
laser powder bed fusion Ti-6Al-4V prior-β grain size stress relief ductility strength-ductility balance
